TL;DR: EXAONE Tabular是一个紧凑的表格基础模型系列，通过上下文学习实现分类和回归预测，无需数据集特定的梯度更新。其核心创新是在每个Transformer层中交织特征轴注意力和基于支持集的条目轴注意力，在4个公开基准上以20.81M参数达到SOTA性能，在TabArena分类排名第一，回归性能媲美1.64B参数的TabFM但推理成本仅为其1/11。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型通常将特征压缩为固定行嵌入后再进行行级学习，这种架构可能丢失特征间的细粒度交互信息。研究者希望通过架构层面的重设计来提升表格上下文学习的预测性能与效率。

Method: 提出了架构中心的表格上下文学习设计：在每个Transformer层内交织两种注意力机制——每个条目内的特征轴注意力，以及每个特征内的基于支持集条件的条目轴注意力，并通过条目摘要和特征摘要token进行中介。模型仅在合成结构因果模型（SCM）先验上预训练，无需针对特定数据集进行梯度更新。

Result: 在TabArena上，20.81M参数的分类模型排名第一，超越调优的集成方法和4小时AutoML流水线；回归性能达到1.64B参数TabFM的水平，但推理成本约为其1/11。在BCCO和TALENT上分类排名第二、回归排名第一。在ScoringBench上，点估计和预测分布质量的平均排名均为最佳，在R²、RMSE和CRPS评估中领先。

Conclusion: EXAONE Tabular确立了作为SOTA紧凑型表格基础模型系列的地位，在分类、点回归和概率回归中均展现强劲预测性能，同时具备高效的模型设计。

Abstract: EXAONE Tabular is a compact tabular foundation model family for classification and regression via in-context learning, producing predictions without dataset-specific gradient updates. Pretrained exclusively on a synthetic structural-causal-model (SCM) prior, its central contribution is an architecture-centered redesign of tabular in-context learning. Rather than compressing features into a fixed row embedding before a separate row-level learner, EXAONE Tabular interleaves feature-axis attention within each item with support-conditioned item-axis attention within each feature at every Transformer layer, mediated by item-summary and feature-summary tokens. Across four public benchmarks, EXAONE Tabular combines strong predictive performance with high efficiency. On TabArena, its 20.81M-parameter classification model ranks first overall, surpassing tuned ensembles and 4-hour AutoML pipelines, while regression reaches the performance regime of the 1.64B-parameter TabFM at roughly 1/11 the inference cost. On BCCO and TALENT, EXAONE Tabular ranks second in classification and first in regression. On ScoringBench, it achieves the best mean rank for both point-estimation and predictive-distribution quality, leading the $R^2$, RMSE, and CRPS evaluations. Together, these results establish EXAONE Tabular as a state-of-the-art compact tabular foundation model family, combining strong predictive performance across classification, point regression, and probabilistic regression with an efficient model design.

</details>


### [299] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFedMARL利用多智能体强化学习（TD3）动态调整联邦学习中的聚合策略，在非IID数据和对抗客户端场景下提升全局模型的准确性、鲁棒性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在客户端数据异构（非IID）时会产生次优且有偏的全局模型，现有的聚合策略难以有效适应这种动态变化的分布差异。

Method: 提出pFedMARL框架，采用服务器端智能体动态调整各客户端的贡献权重以优化全局模型鲁棒性，同时客户端智能体平衡全局和本地更新的比重，实现无需预训练的自适应个性化建模，基于Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3)算法实现。

Result: 在训练半监督音频频谱图Transformer时，pFedMARL在多个非IID场景和存在对抗客户端的情况下，匹配或超越了FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法，显著提升了准确性、鲁棒性和公平性。

Conclusion: pFedMARL通过动态自适应的聚合策略有效解决了联邦学习中非IID数据分布带来的性能退化问题，具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [300] [Geometry-Constrained Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: Learning Edge Geometry via Banach Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25807)
*K S Sesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何约束KAN（geometry-constrained KANs），用Banach对偶映射导出的可学习标量指数p>1代替固定基函数（样条、多项式、傅里叶），每个边学习自己的几何。实验表明在50个符号回归目标上，Banach-KAN中位NRMSE为0.030，与切比雪夫并列、优于样条；在噪声鲁棒性上显著更优（ℓ^p-KAN仅退化3.7×，而未经正则的样条退化21.6×），且在小样本场景下表现最好，学到的指数提供了可解释的目标相关几何信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN变体依赖固定的基函数（样条、多项式、傅里叶等），这些基函数在观察数据之前就强加了一种函数空间几何。作者认为基的选择是KAN的核心，而固定基无法适应不同目标的内在几何结构，因此希望让边缘激活的几何本身也变得可学习。

Method: 从Banach对偶映射导出边缘激活函数族，每个边缘引入一个可学习的标量指数p>1。该指数控制定性行为：次欧氏值（p<2）产生类似ℓ1（LASSO）几何的尖锐阈值行为，p=2恢复线性区，较大p值在原点附近产生更平坦的响应。通过在50个符号回归目标（40个AI Feynman基准+10个合成压力测试）上对比固定基基线进行评估。

Result: 在50个符号回归目标上，Banach-KAN中位NRMSE为0.030，与Chebyshev并列且优于样条；18方程核心的平均排名上Banach-KAN最佳（2.00），完整基准上与最强样条统计持平（2.32 vs 2.34）。噪声鲁棒性方面，σ从0到1时ℓ^p-KAN仅退化3.7×（低于交叉验证样条的≈11×和未正则样条的21.6×），Banach-KAN退化8.8×与调参样条相当但远稳定于未正则者。小样本场景中Banach-KAN获胜次数最多，学到的指数在固定初始化下揭示了跨方程族和输入维度的一致、目标相关的几何排序。

Conclusion: 通过学习每个边缘的Banach几何指数p，KAN可以自适应匹配目标函数的几何结构，在预测精度、噪声鲁棒性和小样本表现上超越或持平固定基基线，同时学到的指数提供了可解释的几何信息。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed activations in deep architectures with learnable univariate edge functions, making the choice of edge parametrisation central. Existing variants rely on fixed bases such as splines, polynomials, or Fourier features, which impose a function-space geometry before data are observed. We introduce geometry-constrained KANs, a family of edge activations derived from Banach duality maps in which the geometry itself is learned through a scalar exponent $p > 1$ per edge. This exponent controls the qualitative response: sub-Euclidean values produce sharp, threshold-like behaviour reminiscent of the $\ell_1$ (LASSO) geometry, $p = 2$ recovers the linear regime, and larger values produce flatter responses near the origin. Across 50 symbolic-regression targets ($40$ from the AI Feynman benchmark plus $10$ synthetic stress tests), geometry-constrained KANs match or beat every fixed-basis baseline on median NRMSE (Banach-KAN $0.030$, tying Chebyshev and improving on splines); on average rank Banach-KAN is best on the $18$-equation core ($2.00$) and statistically tied with the strongest spline on the full benchmark ($2.32$ vs. $2.34$). The clearest gains appear under measurement noise: as $σ$ grows from $0$ to $1$, $\ell^p$-KAN degrades only $3.7\times$ -- below even a cross-validated spline ($\approx 11\times$) -- while an unregularised spline degrades $21.6\times$; Banach-KAN degrades $8.8\times$, comparable to a tuned spline but far more stable than the unregularised one. Banach-KAN also takes the most per-equation wins in the small-sample regime, with fixed-basis models catching up only as the training set grows. Learned exponents provide an interpretable, relative signal: at a fixed initialisation they reveal a consistent, target-dependent geometric ordering across equation families and input dimensions.

</details>


### [301] [Canalization Before Generalization: Grokking as a Dynamical Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25813)
*Yiming Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究过度参数化神经网络中grokking现象下的函数选择机制。通过在grokking平台期扫描短时固定持续时间的权重衰减（WD）脉冲，发现其对后续泛化时间的影响在平台期早期是无序的，但后期形成稳定的剂量排序（更强的WD增加导致更早泛化，更强WD减少导致更晚泛化）。这种排序在可见泛化之前就已出现，同时测试损失屏障坍缩至零，作者将此称为"函数选择的运河化"（canalization of function selection）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度参数化神经网络存在众多在训练数据上表现相同但对未见样本行为迥异的解。Grokking现象将训练拟合与可见泛化分离，为研究训练过程中函数选择如何发展提供了窗口。理解这一选择机制对于揭示泛化的本质规律至关重要。

Method: 在三种grokking任务上，于平台期（plateau）扫描短时、固定持续时间的权重衰减（WD）脉冲，测量脉冲对后续泛化时间（generalization time）的偏移量。通过比较不同强度WD脉冲（正负方向）的影响来分析时序排序结构，并测量扰动检查点与基线泛化检查点之间的测试损失屏障。

Result: 研究发现：1）WD脉冲对泛化时间的影响在平台期早期是无序的，但后期形成稳定的剂量-顺序关系（dose ordering），即更强的WD增加导致更早泛化，更强的WD减少导致更晚泛化；2）这种排序在可见泛化出现之前就已建立，在全部三个任务中均一致；3）在有序时序效应持续存在的同时，扰动与基线泛化检查点之间的测试损失屏障坍缩至接近零。这表明解空间在收敛而时序敏感性保持有序。

Conclusion: 作者将"解选择日益受限"与"剂量有序的时序敏感性持续存在"这一组合现象命名为"函数选择的运河化"（canalization of function selection）。这说明grokking过程中存在一个隐藏的"预排序"阶段：在可见泛化之前，解空间已经沿着某种方向性轨迹收敛，而这一机制与优化景观中平坦性（损失屏障为零）并存，提示泛化的涌现可能由更深层的解空间动力学所驱动。

Abstract: For overparameterized neural networks, many solutions can fit the training data equally well while behaving very differently on unseen samples. Grokking separates training fit from visible generalization, providing a window for studying how this selection develops during training. We sweep short, fixed-duration weight-decay (WD) pulses across this plateau and measure how they shift later generalization time. Across three grokking tasks, these shifts are unordered early in the plateau but later form a stable dose ordering, with stronger WD increases leading to earlier generalization and stronger WD decreases leading to later generalization. This ordering emerges before visible generalization in all three tasks. Meanwhile, test-loss barriers between perturbed and baseline generalization checkpoints collapse toward zero while the ordered timing effects persist. We call this combination of increasingly constrained solution selection and persistent dose-ordered timing sensitivity the canalization of function selection.

</details>


### [302] [Learning Continuous Regional Temperature Fields with Lead-Time and Resolution Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25823)
*Chunlei Shi,Jiong Wang,Yi-Lin Wei,Junming Hou,Jinjin Liu,Yecheng Zhang,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSTF（连续时空温度预报器），将区域近地表温度（T2M）预报建模为查询条件化的连续时空场评估问题，通过把预报时效和输出分辨率作为显式查询输入神经场，实现灵活的逐时预报、中间时效诊断和分辨率可控输出，在东南中国0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上取得最优确定性技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的区域温度预报器通常在预定义网格上产生固定帧集合，难以支持查询依赖的预报时效或显示分辨率的评估需求，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: CSTF首先将多变量ERA5历史数据编码为潜在气象状态，然后解码为基于坐标的T2M神经场。空间位置、预报时效和输出分辨率作为显式查询输入。此外设计了空间梯度、时间差分和尺度一致性三个正则化目标，分别约束区域热力结构、逐时效演化和跨分辨率一致性。

Result: 在东南中国0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上，CSTF取得最优综合确定性预报技能，包括偏差降低17.0%，并通过全局诊断展示了灵活的时效选择和分辨率可控推理能力。

Conclusion: 将预报任务重新定义为查询条件化的连续场评估，能够突破固定输出的限制，在统一框架下实现灵活时效与分辨率可控的T2M预报，同时保持良好的物理一致性。

Abstract: Accurate regional near-surface temperature forecasting is fundamental to short-range weather services and downstream risk assessment. Existing deep learning-based regional forecasters commonly produce a fixed set of future frames on a prescribed grid, limiting their use when forecast products must be evaluated at query-dependent lead times or display resolutions. To overcome these fixed-output constraints, we formulate regional T2M forecasting as query-conditioned continuous spatiotemporal temperature field evaluation and propose the Continuous Spatiotemporal Temperature Forecaster (CSTF), a neural field that turns forecast lead time and output resolution into explicit queries when evaluating 2-m temperature (T2M). Specifically, CSTF first encodes multivariable ERA5 histories into latent meteorological states and then decodes T2M as a coordinate-based field. Accordingly, spatial location, forecast lead time, and output resolution are introduced as queries, enabling standard hourly forecasts, intermediate lead-time diagnostics, and resolution-controllable outputs within a unified field-evaluation framework. Furthermore, to maintain coherence across flexible field queries, we design spatial-gradient, temporal-difference, and scale-consistency objectives that regularize regional thermal structures, lead-wise evolution, and cross-resolution agreement. Experiments on the Southeast China 0-6 h ERA5-Land benchmark demonstrate that CSTF achieves the best aggregate deterministic skill, including a 17.0 percent reduction in Bias, with global-scope diagnostics further illustrating flexible lead-time and resolution-controllable inference.

</details>


### [303] [VINCENT: Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25841)
*Fan-Sheng Chuang,Xuchen Li,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VINCENT框架，一个针对药物协同作用预测的"事后解释"系统，通过注意力/梯度信号提取原子对证据、聚合成化学一致性的基序、并经重复局部扰动验证后反馈优化解释，获得兼具化学一致性、扰动稳定性和与预测器行为对齐的跨药物基序对解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物协同预测中，仅提供单一协同分数不够，研究者还需知道哪些分子区域共同驱动预测。现有可解释协同模型虽有原子或亚结构级信号，但解释内置于预测器架构中，且没有在重复扰动下验证跨药物区域得分，也未将验证证据反馈以细化解释。缺乏可靠、稳定且对齐预测器行为的基序对解释。

Method: VINCENT是一种事后（post-training）可解释框架，面向固定交互感知协同预测器。具体流程：1) 从注意力和梯度信号中提取原子对证据；2) 将原子聚集成化学一致的基序（motif）；3) 通过重复局部扰动验证候选基序对；4) 将验证后的证据反馈以优化基序分配，最终产生满足化学一致性、扰动稳定性、预测器行为对齐三类标准的解释。

Result: 在25对文献标注子集上，VINCENT的基序召回率均值达0.826（95% CI: 0.78-0.87），优于基线的0.49-0.66。在全部71对测试数据上，验证过的交互得分实现了3.36的TP/TN分离度。

Conclusion: 闭环扰动验证机制能比现有替代方案更准确地恢复文献支持的分子区域，同时生成的跨药物交互得分更好地反映预测器内部行为，证明这种"提取-聚合-验证-反馈"的事后解释范式对药物协同解释任务有效。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction estimates whether two drugs produce a stronger joint effect than expected from their individual activities. For drug combination discovery, a single synergy score is often not enough: researchers also need to know which molecular regions jointly drive the prediction. We study motif-pair synergy explanation, which identifies pairs of chemically coherent regions, one from each drug, that jointly contribute to predicted synergy. Existing interpretable synergy models expose atom- or substructure-level signals, but their explanations are built into the predictor architecture, and none validates cross-drug region scores under repeated perturbations or feeds that evidence back to refine the explanation. A reliable motif-pair explanation should instead be chemically coherent, perturbation-stable, and aligned with predictor behavior. We introduce VINCENT (Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics), a post-training framework for a fixed interaction-aware synergy predictor. VINCENT extracts atom-pair evidence from attention and gradient signals, groups atoms into chemically coherent motifs, and validates candidate motif pairs through repeated local perturbations. The validated evidence is fed back to refine motif assignments, yielding explanations that satisfy these three criteria. On a 25-pair literature-annotated subset, VINCENT achieves a mean motif recall of 0.826 (95% CI: 0.78-0.87), compared with 0.49-0.66 for baselines. Across all 71 test pairs, its validated interaction scores yield a TP/TN separation of 3.36. These results show that closed-loop perturbation validation recovers literature-supported molecular regions more accurately than existing alternatives while producing cross-drug interaction scores that better reflect predictor behavior.

</details>


### [304] [CEDAR: Controlled and Event-Driven Demand Forecasting via Residual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25871)
*Junjie Meng,Ranxu Zhang,Zi-an Zhang,Shujun Liu,Xiaoning Qi,Xiaozhou Xu,Yanyong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CEDAR框架，一个两阶段的决策条件化需求预测框架，用于大规模电商市场中受控的事件驱动模拟。第一阶段通过Action-Interleaved Transformer学习动作条件的状态转移，第二阶段利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助的文本表示进行残差校正，解决传统TSF方法的策略不敏感性和自回归惯性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商市场中的预测需要支持规划任务（如预算调度下的销售结果评估），但现有TSF方法本质上是被动的，即使将运营决策作为辅助协变量，也优化的是历史策略下的相关性外推，存在自回归惯性，混淆内生市场演变与决策诱导的转变，导致策略不敏感的滚动预测和不可靠的反事实分析。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I使用Action-Interleaved Transformer学习可控的动作条件状态转移，用于计划干预下的滚动预测；Stage II使用Residual Correction Module，结合外部事件信号和LLM辅助文本表示，对齐嘈杂的事件描述与产品上下文，校正事件驱动的偏差。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴1688的大规模真实数据集（约3200万条产品轨迹，含配对的state-action序列和对齐的事件信号），离线实验和在线生产环境中的受控实验均表明CEDAR在模拟准确性上一致优于强TSF基线，并为实际预算规划带来实用收益。

Conclusion: CEDAR框架有效解决了大规模电商场景中决策条件化需求预测的挑战，通过动作条件化状态转移学习和事件驱动的残差校正，实现了策略敏感的可靠模拟，为规划任务提供了实用支持，并在生产环境中验证了其实际价值。

Abstract: Forecasting in large-scale e-commerce marketplaces is increasingly required to support planning: merchants need to evaluate sales outcomes under future action sequences such as budget schedules, rather than passively predicting what happens next. However, most existing time series forecasting (TSF) approaches remain inherently passive. Even when incorporating operational decisions as auxiliary covariates, they typically optimize for correlation-based extrapolation under historical policies. This design suffers from autoregressive inertia and conflates endogenous market evolution with decision-induced transitions, leading to policy-insensitive rollouts and unreliable counterfactual analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDAR (Controlled and Event-Driven Demand forecasting via Action-aware Residual decomposition), a two-stage framework for robust decision-conditioned simulation. In Stage I, an Action-Interleaved Transformer learns controllable action-conditioned state transitions for rollout under planned interventions. In Stage II, a Residual Correction Module leverages external event signals and LLM-assisted text representations to align noisy event descriptions with product context and correct event-driven deviations. Our study is enabled by a large-scale real-world dataset from Alibaba 1688, comprising approximately 32 million product trajectories with paired state-action sequences and aligned event signals. Extensive offline experiments and online controlled experiments in production demonstrate that CEDAR consistently improves simulation accuracy over strong TSF baselines and delivers practical gains for real-world budget planning.

</details>


### [305] [How Edge of Stability Hinders SCAFFOLD in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25873)
*Anant Khandelwal,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦学习中SCAFFOLD算法在实践上不如FedAvg的原因是"稳定性边缘(EoS)"动态和渐进锐化现象的存在。实验发现EoS-like动态在FedAvg和SCAFFOLD中均普遍存在，且数据异质性会影响锐度平衡值，而SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下对全局梯度的估计能力严重退化，这解释了其在深度学习中的欠佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，数据异质性在理论上会减缓优化速度，为此设计了如SCAFFOLD这样不受数据异质性影响的算法。然而，尽管SCAFFOLD具有强大的理论保证，在实际中却很少超越简单的FedAvg算法。作者旨在揭示这一理论-实践差距的根本原因，以更好地理解联邦优化算法的行为。

Method: 作者通过大量经验探针（extensive empirical probing）研究了联邦优化中的EoS（Edge of Stability）动态和渐进锐化现象。具体包括：(1) 在多种架构和超参数下观察FedAvg和SCAFFOLD的EoS-like动态；(2) 分析学习率和数据异质性对锐度平衡值的影响；(3) 测量SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下对全局梯度估计的误差与锐度之间的相关性。

Result: 研究发现：EoS-like动态在FedAvg和SCAFFOLD中均普遍存在；锐度平衡值与学习率成反比（与GD一致）；数据异质性程度（而非本地步骤数）影响平衡值；最重要的是，SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下对全局梯度的估计能力严重下降，其估计误差与锐度高度相关。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在深度学习中的表现不如FedAvg的机制可以归结为：当优化进入EoS状态时，高锐度导致SCAFFOLD无法可靠地估计全局梯度，从而丧失了其在理论上的优势。这为理解联邦优化算法的动态行为以及在实践场景下设计更有效的算法提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [306] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）这一方法进行了系统性综述，聚焦其在数学推理任务中的核心失败模式——推理路径坍缩（collapse），并提出一个三杠杆分析框架（where/what/when）来统一理解该问题的成因与调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通过让模型以自身为教师，利用特权信息（如参考答案、规划或环境反馈）提供逐token稠密监督，早期结果良好，但该方法的信号不对称性会导致推理路径逐步缩窄的"坍缩"问题，这一问题已成为该领域的主导性失败模式。然而不同论文对这一现象使用了不同术语且理解分散，需要结构化梳理。

Method: 本文为综述性论文，不报告新实验。采用结构性分析框架，将坍缩视为受三个杠杆共同支配的症状：(i) 信号应用位置（token加权方式）；(ii) 教师所见信息（特权信息性质）；(iii) 信号变化时机（教师动态与指导衰减）。范围限定于数学推理领域。

Result: 本文不报告新实验结果，其贡献在于：建立了跨论文的统一词汇表，将不同论文中以不同名称描述的现象统一归入坍缩这一框架；明确了哪些现象已被充分解决、哪些仍存在争议；论证了特权信息会加剧坍缩，但坍缩并非OPSD所特有。

Conclusion: 坍缩问题是OPSD领域的主导性失败模式，可通过三个杠杆进行治理。对坍缩的理解需要结构化的共同语言，而非各自为政的命名。数学推理是该方法的最佳测试场，但需要在"已经解决"与"仍有争议"之间划定清晰边界。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [307] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将分子基础模型Uni-Mol2在GS-LF多标签气味描述符预测基准上微调，评估其在四个互补下游任务（跨数据集气味描述符预测、有气/无气分类、对映体评估、气味混合物可辨别性）上的迁移能力。结果显示微调模型在GS-LF基准上匹配或超越现有最优气味专用基线，且一致地迁移到各下游任务，支持"一次训练、多任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在回答一个关键问题：在单一典型嗅觉预测任务上微调的分子基础模型，能否学习到可迁移到多种机器嗅觉问题的通用表征？这一问题的回答将决定机器嗅觉领域是否可以采用更高效、更通用的训练范式。

Method: 作者将Uni-Mol2在GS-LF基准上进行微调（用于多标签气味描述符预测），随后在不进行额外深度学习训练的情况下，在四个互补的下游场景中评估该模型：跨数据集气味描述符预测、气味与非气味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物可辨别性。此外，通过对映体分析对比了三维分子表征与二维图模型对镜像分子区分能力的差异。

Result: 微调后的模型在GS-LF主基准上匹配或超越了当前最优的气味专用基线，并在所有下游评估中表现出一致的迁移能力。对映体分析表明三维分子表征能够区分镜像分子，而二维图模型从根本上无法做到这一点，但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是未解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持"一次训练、跨任务迁移"的机器嗅觉新范式，并表明化学预训练的分子表征为可迁移的气味预测提供了坚实基础。同时指出立体化学感知预测仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [308] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICON分解方法，用于量化神经网络层中每个概念在控制其他所有概念和结局变量后所解释的方差比例，从而更准确地识别模型真正依赖的概念，避免概念间相关性导致的虚假结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的可解释性方法逐个独立地测试概念是否可从网络层中解码，但忽略了概念之间的相关性，可能将概念间的相关误解为模型确实使用了这些概念的证据，导致错误的捷径学习检测结论。

Method: ICON分解通过方差分解的方式，量化每个概念在考虑了所有其他概念和结局变量之后所能解释的层方差比例。在合成数据上使用已知真值进行验证，并在皮肤病变和脑成像模型上进行实际应用。

Result: 在合成数据上，ICON比七种替代基线方法更准确地恢复概念重要性。在实际应用中，它能隔离模型真正依赖的概念、量化未被任何给定概念解释的表征，并产生稀疏解释，通过重训练和分布外测试得到验证。

Conclusion: ICON分解提供了一种更可靠的捷径学习检测方法，能够通过条件方差分解来正确区分概念间的相关性与模型对概念的真正依赖。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [309] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论视角重新审视时间序列可解释性，提出一个统一的目标函数，将归因解释与反事实解释桥接在单一框架中，基于信息瓶颈原理设计了一个名为\modelname{}的参数化变换网络框架，该框架通过构造嵌入解释的实例来同时生成忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释，在合成与真实基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列解释方法分为归因解释和反事实解释两大类别，但二者被独立研究：归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法存在严重不稳定性、易产生类似对抗性噪声的伪解释。此外，现有解释器容易陷入平凡解并受分布偏移影响。

Method: 提出一个统一的面向可解释时间序列学习的目标函数，将归因推理与反事实推理整合在同一框架内。该目标函数基于信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）原理构建，显式防止平凡解释和分布外反事实。基于此目标函数，设计了\modelname{}框架——通过学习一个参数化变换网络来构造嵌入解释的实例，其中保留的信息生成归因解释，受控的信息移除生成稳定的反事实解释。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界基准上，\modelname{}与多个最先进的基线方法进行对比，大量定量和定性实验结果表明\modelname{}始终优于竞争方法，能产生忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释。

Conclusion: 通过信息论视角的统一框架，归因与反事实解释可以相互补充而非彼此孤立，从而同时解决归因缺乏因果验证和反事实不稳定的问题，推动时间序列可解释性研究向更严谨、更可靠的方向发展。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [310] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共享骨干网络、按任务自由路由的最后一层信念（per-task free-routed last-layer belief）的组合似然方法，用于随机动力系统的多任务预测。通过在学习似然参数时吸收单位相关的损失缩放，在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63、真实空气质量数据）上取得了最优单次运行NLL和校准性能，且在计算成本上远低于调参网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力系统的预测通常需要同时预测多个可观测对象（未来状态、阈值事件、机制标签），每个都有自己的似然。标准的多数任务学习需要针对每个任务的损失进行调参或学习，繁琐且昂贵。作者希望将观测对象的似然组合起来，用自由路由的最后一层信念在共享骨干网络上实现，自动吸收单位相关的损失缩放，避免显式调参。

Method: 在共享骨干网络上，为每个任务使用自由路由的最后一层信念（free-routed last-layer beliefs）来组合各观测对象的似然。单位相关的损失缩放被吸收进似然参数中，并在同一梯度更新中学习。利用随机动力学提供可计算的预测方差作为真实值（ground truth），从而直接比较预测分布的质量。

Result: 在良设定、同方差（homoscedastic）的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程上，学习到的预测法则恢复了解析核，与正确设定的基线打平。在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63和真实空气质量数据）上，信念的输入相关方差起到了区分作用：在状态和机制任务上取得最佳单次运行NLL，校准性能优于其NLL所击败的arms，且仅需调参网格的一小部分计算成本。在真实序列上，状态边际在五个滚动起点（rolling origins）上保持。

Conclusion: 该方法在良设定场景下与理论预期一致，在异方差系统上通过输入相关的信念方差提升了预测性能与校准度，同时大幅降低了调参成本，是一种高效且理论上可解释的多任务预测似然组合方案。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [311] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个任务依赖的理论框架，用于分析Transformer注意力中低秩适配（LoRA）更新在不同秩下能达到的近似误差。通过谱界和概率界，给出了期望KL误差的上下界，并揭示softmax饱和可能使匹配注意力函数所需的秩小于匹配有限logits所需的秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA更新的秩（rank）选择传统上依赖经验调参，缺乏理论指导。作者希望提供一种任务依赖的理论，能够根据下游任务的输入分布和目标注意力函数，预测每个LoRA秩下可实现的近似误差，从而为秩选择提供理论基础。

Method: 作者固定预训练的注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务的输入分布，从理论上界定了秩为r的query LoRA更新所能达到的最小期望KL误差。推导了与注意力分数差d相关的ψ函数下界和上界；在可实现性、几何和矩条件下，用尾部能量T_r来界定最优秩误差；还提供了目标Fisher界、不受限制的下界，以及融合多头LoRA和联合query/key更新的扩展分析。

Result: 得到了下界误差正比于ψ(‖d‖₂)和上界min{‖d‖₂²/4,√2‖d‖₂}；在可实现性条件下，误差介于显式的ψ(√T_r)倍数和min{T_r/4,√(2T_r)}之间。发现softmax饱和可构造出使匹配注意力函数所需秩小于匹配有限logits所需秩的实例。扩展分析揭示了多头LoRA中秩共享和query/key因子分解约束的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为LoRA秩选择提供了第一个任务依赖的谱理论基础，表明所需秩由下游加权尾部能量决定，并揭示了注意力函数（经softmax后）与有限logits在秩需求上的差异，以及多头共享秩和query/key联合更新的谱效应。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [312] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子启发的驾驶人行为表征框架，将每个驾驶人编码为随时间演化的密度矩阵，以统一方式捕捉行为的连续性、概率性、情境依赖性和历史依赖性。该框架在I-24 MOTION数据集上无监督训练，恢复了自由流、过渡和拥堵三种可解释的驾驶模式，复现了宏观交通现象（基本图和滞后环），并可向经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，为自动驾驶提供周围驾驶人的实时行为读取和短期运动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶人行为具有异质性、情境依赖性和时变性，这些特性塑造了观察到的交通现象。然而，大多数模型事先固定行为变量之间的交互方式和形式，超出该形式的行为被当作噪声吸收；而能灵活捕捉这些行为的模型又往往失去可解释性。因此需要一个既能灵活建模复杂行为又保持可解释性的统一框架。

Method: 将每个驾驶人编码为一个随时间演化的密度矩阵，融合了行为不确定性、时间演化和情境依赖的行为变化。框架在I-24 MOTION数据集上以无监督方式训练，学习行为变量间的交互关系。该表征可为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并为自动驾驶提供周围驾驶人的实时行为读取与短期运动预测。

Result: 框架恢复了三种可解释的驾驶模式（自由流、过渡、拥堵），捕捉了数据的行为范围和驾驶人在条件变化时的平滑模式转换。该表征复现了已知宏观现象，与基本图一致并再现了滞后环。同时支持实际应用：为经典跟驰模型提供参数，以及为自动驾驶提供实时行为读取和短期预测。

Conclusion: 该框架指向了一种本质上可解释且可信赖的交通建模方式，并发布了涵盖数据处理、训练、推断和分析的开源工具包。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [313] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后验模型剪枝对稀疏自动编码器（SAEs）可靠性的影响，理论上证明剪枝对SAE的影响由协方差加权的扰动能量决定；发现幅度剪枝忽略激活几何导致严重退化，而激活感知方法（Wanda、SparseGPT）更鲁棒；并揭示中间层对剪枝更敏感的结构性弱点，据此提出逐层稀疏度分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAEs被广泛用于解释大型语言模型的内部表示，但在后验模型压缩（剪枝）情境下的可靠性尚未被充分理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究剪枝如何影响SAE行为，并寻找更鲁棒的剪枝策略。

Method: 1) 理论上推导出剪枝对固定SAE的影响受扰动能量（协方差加权范数）控制；2) 对比幅度剪枝与激活感知方法（Wanda和SparseGPT）在保留SAE行为上的效果；3) 揭示所有剪枝方法共有的结构脆弱性——中间层比早期/晚期层对剪枝更敏感；4) 基于此提出逐层稀疏度分配策略；5) 在四种模型架构上进行实验验证。

Result: 幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何而扭曲表示空间并降低SAE功能；激活感知方法通过隐式控制扰动能量更为鲁棒；所有剪枝方法中中间层均更敏感；提出的逐层稀疏度分配策略在相同平均剪枝稀疏度下实现更低的困惑度；四种模型架构上的实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: SAE在剪枝下的可靠性取决于剪枝方法对激活几何的感知程度，激活感知剪枝方法显著优于幅度剪枝；利用中间层更敏感的结构性发现，可通过逐层稀疏度分配在保持压缩的同时更好地维持SAE的语义解释功能。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [314] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过光谱探针分析揭示正交优化器（如Muon）加速大语言模型预训练的机制，并提出光谱感知优化器SAMuon及其轻量版本SAMuon-lite，在多种模型规模和批大小下均优于AdamW和Muon基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交优化器（如Muon）相比Adam能显著加速大语言模型预训练，但其加速机制尚未被完全理解，这限制了进一步改进优化器的能力。

Method: 在真实训练轨迹的检查点上，对每个动量缓冲区进行奇异值分解，并在留出数据上估计每个奇异方向上的损失最优步长，构建光谱分配画像。基于该画像发现Muon对"骨干"（bulk）区域的缩放不足，据此引入SAMuon：保留头部（head）的Muon缩放，使用静态光谱先验放大骨干区域。提供两个变体：完整的SAMuon使用低秩随机化SVD，SAMuon-lite使用秩一幂迭代的两级近似。

Result: 光谱画像具有各向异性但在批次和训练阶段间稳定，且跨优化器和模型规模一致：不稳定的头部需要更小的步长，而容忍度高的骨干允许更大的步长。SAMuon相比Muon节省13.3%至24.0%的训练token达到相同验证损失，SAMuon-lite几乎保留全部增益且几乎无额外墙钟开销。两个变体在124M到1B参数的所有评估配置中均超越调优的AdamW和Muon基线。

Conclusion: 光谱分配分析为Muon为何优于Adam而Adam优于SGD提供了统一解释，同时揭示了Muon均匀缩放对骨干区域的利用不足。SAMuon及其轻量版本通过静态光谱先验放大了骨干区域，在不增加持久优化器状态和显著额外FLOPs的情况下实现更优的样本效率，且理想化的精确白化版本保持Muon的渐近收敛率。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [315] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DualOPSD，一种非对称交替自蒸馏框架，同时适应学生模型和特权教师模型的策略。与固定特权教师的OPSD不同，DualOPSD先让学生向特权教师学习，然后教师沿同一学生轨迹向更新后的学生分布移动，使后续监督对学习者具有响应性且无需额外rollout。在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上分别比OPSD提升23.61、13.89和10.00个avg@12百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD使用学生模型的特权副本提供密集监督，但特权教师在训练过程中保持固定不变，而学生的分布和输出风格在不断变化。固定的教师监督无法适应学习者的演化，导致监督信号与学生当前分布脱节，限制了蒸馏效果。

Method: DualOPSD采用非对称交替框架：第一阶段学生从特权教师学习；第二阶段教师在相同的学生轨迹上向更新后的学生分布移动。这种双向更新使教师监督对学习者具有响应性，且不需要额外的rollout成本。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD相比OPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上分别提升23.61、13.89和10.00个avg@12百分点。1.7B和4B规模的结果显示精度提升依赖于模型规模。在三种规模下DualOPSD均减少了截断（truncation）。4B规模的诊断实验表明师生之间的双向KL散度更低。

Conclusion: DualOPSD通过让特权教师沿学生轨迹自适应更新，显著优于固定教师的OPSD，验证了交替式师生共同适应策略的有效性。精度提升随模型规模增大而增强，且在所有规模下均能减少截断现象，同时降低师生分布间的KL散度。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [316] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust CurveMoE，一种高效的混合专家框架，通过低损耗路径连接不同扰动范数特化的模型，利用其互补的鲁棒性特征，在降低训练成本的同时提升多范数对抗防御的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多范数对抗防御方法通常在单一参数配置下优化相互竞争的鲁棒性目标，导致训练成本高昂且存在不利的鲁棒性权衡。需要一种能够高效协调不同范数防御目标的框架。

Method: Robust CurveMoE包含四个关键组件：(1)从鲁棒性约束的曲线位置提取干净与范数特化专家；(2)仅对有影响力的层进行选择性专家化，其余参数在各路由路径间共享；(3)通过初始化梯度分数选择有影响力的曲线参数，实现贡献引导的部分更新以降低曲线构建成本；(4)理论上界定了部分优化与全曲线优化之间的目标差距。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上使用WideResNet和Vision Transformer架构进行实验，Robust CurveMoE在干净准确率、范数特化准确率和Union准确率上均优于MSD和ERMC。在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上分别将Union准确率提升了2.37和2.13个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验和消融研究验证了部分更新、选择性专家化以及鲁棒性约束专家选择的有效性，表明Robust CurveMoE是一种高效且有效的多范数对抗防御方案。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [317] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Channel Group-Shared (CGS)低秩近似方法，基于SVD分解的参数共享策略来压缩大核CNN中点卷积的参数规模，解决边缘设备存储和内存加载瓶颈，实现大核CNN在资源受限设备上的可行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN通过扩大感受野获得卓越性能，但参数呈二次增长，严重阻碍边缘部署。现有高效架构虽然通过深度可分离卷积等技术压缩深度卷积，但忽略了点卷积才是参数量的主要来源（如RepLKNet-31B中点卷积占87%以上），导致在4-12GB内存的智能设备上存在高昂的存储成本和严重的内存加载限制。

Method: CGS低秩近似是一种基于SVD分解的参数共享策略。具体地，该方法构建与SVD分解同构的结构化低秩范式，包含跨通道组共享的下/上投影矩阵（高参数成本）以及各通道组专属的可缩放对角矩阵（低参数成本）。这种组共享设计实现了显著的参数缩减。

Result: 在RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK等多种大核CNN上验证，CGS增强后的模型在保持竞争性性能的同时大幅降低了存储成本，并且通过缓解存储约束、降低加载时的内存带宽压力、减少模型加载延迟，实现了预训练大核CNN在边缘设备上的可行部署。

Conclusion: CGS通过针对大核CNN中占参数主导地位的点卷积进行SVD-based参数共享压缩，成功弥合了高性能视觉模型与边缘设备实际部署之间的鸿沟，实现了性能和存储效率之间经验上有利的平衡。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [318] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TraceML是一个版本级别的数据集，记录了4,465个人类Kaggle轨迹和207个Agent轨迹（在134个比赛中），配对的实验揭示了LLM智能体在自主机器学习开发中的核心缺陷：Agent在狭窄循环中坍缩（如反复调权重或原地变异模型），缺乏人类专家的工作切换与回溯能力。基于人类实践提炼的规划提示虽能部分改善行为，但无法完全弥合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有outcome-based基准只能记录LLM与人类之间的能力差距，却无法解释差距的成因，因为它们只评估最终提交结果而丢弃了开发过程。为了深入理解AI智能体在自主机器学习开发中落后的根本原因，需要一个能够捕捉完整开发轨迹（版本、分数、时间戳、行为标签）的数据集。

Method: 构建TraceML数据集：在版本级别schema下配对记录人类与Agent在同一Kaggle比赛中的工作轨迹。包含4,465条人类轨迹（134个比赛）、430条配对人类轨迹和207条Agent轨迹（7个比赛由两个Agent框架完成）。每个代码版本记录分数、时间戳以及行为标签（动作、意图、编辑规模、分数影响）。通过对比分析人类与Agent的行为分布差异，并设计从人类实践中提炼的规划提示来干预Agent行为。

Result: 发现专家交替进行数据处理、验证、模型更改和集成，并会回头重试已放弃的方法；而Codex Agent陷入重新加权集成和调整提交的循环，MLEvolve原地变异模型，两者均缺乏人类水平的转向速度和回溯频率。引入人类实践提炼的规划提示后，Agent的相应行为向人类特征靠近且分数提升，但整体的努力分布仍呈Agent形态——指令只能弥合可归结为指令的那部分差距。

Conclusion: 通过版本级行为轨迹分析，将LLM智能体在ML开发中落后于人类的能力差距具体化为可观察的行为模式（狭窄循环坍缩、缺乏转向与回溯），并证明仅靠提示工程（规划提示）只能部分弥合差距——即那些可以还原为指令的差距部分，暗示剩余差距需要更深层的训练或架构层面的改变。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


### [319] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了机器学习算法设计中手工设计的各个环节（架构、损失函数、训练方案）可完全交由自主AI代理完成。采用autoresearch协议，AI编码代理编辑训练脚本、运行固定预算实验，并根据单一不可变指标保留或丢弃修改。在81次无人值守实验（26小时）中，代理在求和最小百分位速率功率控制这个非凸、非光滑、强NP难的问题上，达到收敛的MM参考解99.5%的性能，推理成本降低约600倍，并且一个参数集适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线资源管理的机器学习算法设计需要大量人工劳动，包括架构、损失函数和训练方案的设计都依赖人工指定。作者希望将这一设计层完全交给自主代理，减少人工干预。

Method: 采用autoresearch协议：AI编码代理对训练脚本进行编辑，运行固定预算实验，根据单一不可变指标决定保留或丢弃更改。代理拥有对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数和任务采样律的完全控制权。同时采用hash固定评估器、强制推理契约和预注册验证器作为安全措施保障结果可信。

Result: 在81次无人值守实验（26小时）中，代理在一次固定成本推理中达到收敛MM参考解的99.5%，推理成本降低约600倍，从第一个可工作架构闭合了94%的差距，且一套参数适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。发现的输出参数化在最小百分位处复现了精确的最大-最小最优分配，对每个训练权重值都成立。

Conclusion: 自主AI代理能够完全接管机器学习算法设计层，不仅达到接近参考方法的性能，还恢复了可证明的结构而非调优常数，展示了自主设计在无线资源管理中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [320] [GaussVLA: Geometry-Aware Spatial Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24959)
*Md Selim Sarowar,Md Tanvir Islam,Sungho Kim,Sangtae Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussVLA是一种基于Mamba的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过引入高斯空间分词器（GST）将语义和深度特征提升为紧凑的3D高斯标记，并结合深度感知链式思维（DA-CoT）进行非自回归的几何推理，在LIBERO基准上以仅200M参数达到93.5%平均成功率、空间任务100%成功率，比SpatialVLA相对提升19.7%且参数效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉观察编码为扁平的2D patch token，缺乏内在几何结构；而密集单目深度仅注入逐像素标量值，无法编码表面朝向或几何置信度，导致策略在动作预测中缺乏结构化的空间推理能力。

Method: GaussVLA包含两个核心模块：(1) 高斯空间分词器（GST）：将冻结的语义和深度特征提升为紧凑的3D高斯token，并使用学习查询对几何显著区域进行池化；(2) Depth-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT)：在语言和流时间条件下进行结构化的非自回归几何推理。整体架构基于Mamba构建。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，GaussVLA以仅200M参数达到93.5%平均成功率和Spatial套件100%成功率，相比SpatialVLA相对平均成功率提升19.7%，同时参数效率显著更高；在模拟和真实世界评估中均表现出强大的空间操作性能。

Conclusion: 通过将3D高斯token与结构化几何推理机制融入Mamba架构，GaussVLA在保持参数高效的同时显著提升了VLA模型的空间操作能力，验证了显式几何表征对机器人操纵策略的增益。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models encode visual observations as flat 2D patch tokens that carry no intrinsic geometric structure, and augmenting them with dense monocular depth injects per-pixel scalar values that encode neither surface orientation nor geometric confidence. This leaves the policy with limited structured spatial reasoning for action prediction. We propose GaussVLA, a Mamba-based VLA that incorporates two custom modules: Gaussian Spatial Tokenizer (GST) to lift frozen semantic and depth features into compact 3D Gaussian tokens, pools geometrically salient regions with learned queries, and \emph{Depth-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT)} that performs structured, non-autoregressive geometric reasoning under language and flow-time conditioning. Across both simulation and real-world evaluations, GaussVLA demonstrates strong spatial-manipulation performance while remaining parameter-efficient. On LIBERO, it achieves 93.5% average success and 100.0% success on the Spatial suite with only 200M parameters, improving over SpatialVLA by 19.7% relative average success while remaining significantly more parameter-efficient.

</details>


### [321] [ROS2 Connect: A new ROS2 over WAN Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25102)
*Daniel Schott,Lakshminarasimhan Srinivasan,Christian Herrmann,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ROS2 Connect，一个基于WebSocket的通信框架，使ROS2系统能够在广域网环境中透明、安全地进行交互，无需修改网络基础设施或DDS配置，显著优于DDS Router、rosbridge和Zenoh等现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS2的通信架构基于DDS和RTPS协议，依赖多播发现机制，而这些机制在广域网环境中通常不可用，导致远程操作分布式机器人系统面临巨大挑战。现有解决方案在延迟、稳定性和可扩展性方面存在不足，亟需一种不依赖多播且能适应WAN环境的通信方案。

Method: 提出一种基于WebSocket的客户端-服务器架构，支持topic、service、action和系统数据的双向交换，并集成身份验证与访问控制机制。该方案无需修改网络基础设施或DDS配置，即可实现跨路由网络的ROS2交互。

Result: 在真实WAN连接上的实验评估表明，与DDS Router、rosbridge和Zenoh等现有解决方案相比，ROS2 Connect具有更低的延迟、更高的稳定性和更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: ROS2 Connect为广域网上的远程操作和分布式机器人应用提供了可靠的基础设施支撑，可作为远程机器人系统通信的实用解决方案。

Abstract: The Robot Operating System 2 (ROS2) has become a widely adopted framework for the development of distributed robotic systems. However, its communication architecture, based on DDS and RTPS, relies on multicast discovery mechanisms that are typically unavailable in wide-area network (WAN) environments, making remote operation challenging. This work presents ROS2 Connect, a WebSocket-based communication framework that enables transparent and secure ROS2 interaction across routed networks without requiring modifications to network infrastructure or DDS configurations. The proposed client-server architecture supports bidirectional exchange of topics, services, actions, and system data while integrating authentication and access control mechanisms. Experimental evaluation over a real WAN connection demonstrates significantly lower latency, higher stability, and improved scalability compared to existing solutions, including DDS Router, rosbridge and Zenoh. Initial results show that ROS2 Connect provides a reliable foundation for teleoperation and distributed robotics applications over wide-area networks.

</details>


### [322] [Extending Ground-Constraint LiDAR-IMU Calibration to Tilted Surfaces in a Continuous-Time Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25135)
*Vassili Korotkine,Pierre Chamoun,Mohammed Ayman Shalaby,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种扩展的无目标LiDAR-IMU标定方法，使其适用于非平坦地面环境。针对地面车辆在正常行驶中无法充分激励传感器系统、以及现有方法假设重力与地面法向量共线而仅限于平坦地面的局限性，提出不依赖该假设的地面平面残差，支持倾斜表面上平面运动的标定。在Husky地面车辆数据集、M2DGR数据集及越野车辆数据集上验证，倾斜与平坦地面场景下的标定重复性均有提升，尤其倾斜场景改善显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR-IMU标定要求传感器系统充分激励，但地面车辆正常运行时仅呈现退化的平面运动，难以满足要求。现有基于平面运动的方法通过假设重力与物理表面法向量共线来构建残差，这使其仅适用于平坦地面。实际应用（如越野环境）中地面往往是倾斜的，该假设不成立，导致标定精度下降。因此需要一种不依赖平坦地面假设的标定方法。

Method: 提出新颖的地面平面残差设计，该残差不依赖重力与地面法向量共线的假设，从而适用于倾斜表面上的平面运动。通过对地面平面约束进行重新建模，使标定过程在倾斜地面场景下依然保持有效。实验采用Husky地面车辆采集数据、M2DGR公开数据集以及越野车辆数据集进行验证。

Result: 在倾斜地面与平坦地面两种场景下，标定重复性均有提升；尤其在倾斜地面场景中，重复性改善显著。代码与实验已开源。

Conclusion: 所提出的地面平面残差方法有效突破了现有LiDAR-IMU标定对平坦地面的限制，可在非平坦（倾斜）地面环境下实现可靠标定，拓展了该方法在越野及复杂地形中的应用范围。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel method that extends targetless LiDAR-IMU calibration for ground vehicles to non- flat environments. Calibration typically necessitates full exci- tation of the sensor rig, a requirement that is not fulfilled by ground vehicles in normal operation. To address the degenerate planar motion, state-of-the-art methods propose residuals that assume the colinearity of the gravity and physical surface normal vectors, restricting usage to cases where the ground is assumed flat. This paper proposes ground-plane residuals that do not require this assumption, and are applicable for planar motion on a tilted surface. Results are demonstrated on a dataset collected from a Husky ground vehicle, on the M2DGR dataset, as well as on an offroad vehicle dataset. Repeatability is shown to be improved both in tilted and flat-ground scenarios, with strong improvement demonstrated for the tilted case. The implementation and experiments are open-sourced at https://github.com/vkorotkine/licalib_tilted_ground.

</details>


### [323] [SkyDrive: Learning to Drive in a New City from Aerial Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25142)
*Weijiang Xiong,Lan Feng,Alexandre Alahi,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SkyDrive框架，利用无人机航拍交通监控作为新环境下自动驾驶轨迹规划器的数据来源。基于137小时航拍监控提取65万个驾驶样本构建基准，零样本实验显示跨城市域差异可通过每地点30分钟的航拍监督显著缓解，证明航拍交通监控是适应自动驾驶系统的高效可扩展数据源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶通过模仿学习在海量人类演示数据上取得了显著进展，但训练好的规划器在零样本迁移到新环境时，由于交通法规、道路布局和驾驶行为等域差异，性能会严重退化。传统解决方案需要在新城市用传感器套件进行资源密集型的本地数据采集。本文旨在寻找一种可扩展、高效替代方案来提供新环境下的驾驶行为监督。

Method: SkyDrive框架利用无人机航拍交通监控为新环境中的自动驾驶智能体提供监督信号。与车载数据采集只记录自车及周围环境不同，航拍平台能在扩展视野内同时观察多个道路使用者，使得每辆车都能成为带有真实驾驶行为的数据来源。基于137小时航拍监控素材提取了65万个驾驶样本并构建了轨迹规划器和运动预测器基准。

Result: 零样本实验使用多种模型揭示了显著的跨城市域差距，但这些差距大多可以通过来自天空的有限监督得到缓解，例如每个地点仅需30分钟的航拍监控即可显著改善性能。

Conclusion: 航拍交通监控是新城市自动驾驶系统适应的高效且可扩展的数据来源。数据与代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has made remarkable progress through imitation learning with massive human demonstration data. However, a trained planner often degrades severely when applied to a new environment zero-shot, because of domain shifts in traffic regulations, road layout and driving behaviors. Therefore, adapting a trajectory planner to a new city typically requires resource-demanding local data collection with a vehicle sensor suite. In this work, we show that driving behavior can be learned from a scalable and efficient alternative. We introduce \emph{SkyDrive}, a framework that utilizes drone-based traffic monitoring to provide efficient supervision for autonomous driving agents in a new environment. While vehicle-based data collection logs the ego and its surroundings, an aerial platform naturally observes many road users simultaneously over an extended field of view. As a result, every vehicle can be a data source with grounded driving behavior, effectively scaling up the amount of supervision. Based on 137 hours of aerial traffic monitoring footage, we extract 650K driving samples and construct a benchmark for trajectory planners and motion predictors. Zero-shot experiments with multiple models reveal significant cross-city domain gaps, but many of them can be alleviated by limited supervision from the sky, e.g., 30 minutes of monitoring per location. Our findings show that aerial traffic monitoring is an efficient and scalable data source for adapting autonomous driving systems in new cities. Data and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [324] [Sequential Object Placement Optimization with Convex Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25162)
*Yuezhe Zhang,Xiangyu Lyu,Sohan Rudra,Davide Tateo,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SOPO-CD，一个将机器人物体装箱问题转化为可微非线性优化问题的顺序优化框架，通过将凸物体放置在凸包内的约束化简为顶点约束，在毫秒级内实现最优放置，相比经典网格搜索方法快100倍，并泛化到2D七巧板、2D俄罗斯方块、3D装箱及真实机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人物体装箱在物流和工业中至关重要，但由于组合搜索的维度灾难以及处理不规则形状物体的动态和接触约束的困难，一直是一个核心挑战。现有启发式和基于学习的方法假设空间采用有限的离散化分辨率，随着离散化精度提高，计算效率急剧下降。作者希望消除这些假设，提出一种更高效、更精确的求解方法。

Method: SOPO-CD框架将物体放置建模为分解自由空间中的可微非线性优化问题。关键理论贡献是证明了凸物体放置在凸包内的本质约束等价于物体顶点位于凸包内。该约束及其导数具有闭式表达形式，单次计算仅需200纳秒。作者实现了自定义求解器，在紧密受限空间中毫秒级内获得最优放置，并提供100倍于经典网格搜索方法的加速比。

Result: 框架泛化到2D七巧板、2D俄罗斯方块和3D装箱问题，展示了强大的计算性能和装箱利用率。此外，在实际场景中使用Allegro Hand和Xarm机器人在线解决了真实世界的七巧板拼图任务。

Conclusion: SOPO-CD通过消除离散化假设、利用凸集顶点约束的闭式可微形式，显著提升了机器人装箱的求解速度和实用性，并验证了其在仿真与真实机器人平台上的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic object packing has been a core challenge for robotic deployment in logistics, industry, etc., due to the curse of dimensionality in combinatorial search and the difficulty of dealing with dynamic and contact constraints for irregularly shaped objects. Current heuristic and learning-based methods assume a limited spatial discretization resolution of space, and computation becomes extremely inefficient as discretization accuracy increases. In this work, we eliminate these assumptions by introducing SOPO-CD, a sequential optimization framework that frames object placement as a differentiable nonlinear optimization problem in a decomposed free space. We prove that placing a convex object inside a convex hull is essentially constraining the vertices of the object inside the convex hull. The constraints and their derivatives can be written in closed form and calculated within $200$ns. We implement a custom solver that achieves optimal placement within tightly constrained space in milliseconds; a $100 \times$ speedup compared to a classical grid search method. We generalize our framework to 2D Tangram, 2D Tetris, and 3D Bin Packing, and have demonstrated strong computational performance and packing utility. We also demonstrate solving a real-world Tangram puzzle online using an Allegro Hand and an Xarm.

</details>


### [325] [Control-Oriented Learning for Dynamic Tracking and Stability Analysis of Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25171)
*Nithin S. Kumar,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向控制的气动软体执行器数据驱动建模与控制框架，将执行器行为分解为非线性静态平衡模型和线性残差动力学模型（基于EDMDc辨识），实现前馈补偿、任务空间反馈控制及局部闭环稳定性分析。实验在低速（~10 mm/s）下达到约1 mm RMSE，高速（~100 mm/s）下低于10 mm RMSE，并能稳定跟踪峰值加速度超过25 m/s²的动态参考轨迹，同时进行实时避障。稳定性分析经实验验证可准确预测稳定、临界和不稳定运行区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动执行器因其高度非线性、分布式的动力学特性，难以建模和控制，尽管其具有固有的柔顺性和安全交互优势。现有方法缺乏兼具准确性和实用性的控制框架，需要一种能够支持稳定性分析和实时控制的结构化建模方案。

Method: 将执行器行为分解为两部分：(1) 非线性静态平衡模型用于前馈补偿；(2) 通过扩展动态模式分解（EDMDc）辨识的线性残差动力学模型用于反馈控制和稳定性分析。两者结合构成增广线性模型，支持任务空间反馈控制、前馈补偿和局部闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 低速（约10 mm/s）轨迹跟踪RMSE约1 mm；高速（约100 mm/s）下RMSE低于10 mm；对峰值加速度超过25 m/s²的用户动态参考轨迹实现稳定跟踪并同步完成实时避障。稳定性分析经实验验证，可准确预测稳定、临界及不稳定运行区域。

Conclusion: 结构化、面向控制的学习方法为软体执行器控制提供了既准确又实用的框架，解决了软体执行器在建模和控制上的核心挑战。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators offer inherent compliance and safe interaction but remain difficult to model and control because of their highly nonlinear, distributed dynamics. We present a control-oriented data-driven modeling and control framework that decomposes actuator behavior into a nonlinear static equilibrium model and a linear residual dynamics model identified using Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition with control (EDMDc). This representation enables feedforward compensation, task-space feedback control, and local closed-loop stability analysis through an augmented linear model. Experiments achieve approximately 1 mm root mean square error (RMSE) during low-speed (approximately 10 mm/s) trajectory tracking and below 10 mm RMSE at higher speeds (approximately 100 mm/s). The framework further achieves stable tracking of highly dynamic user-generated references with peak accelerations exceeding 25 m/s^2 while simultaneously performing real-time obstacle avoidance. Finally, the proposed stability analysis is experimentally validated by accurately predicting stable, marginal, and unstable operating regimes. These results demonstrate that structured, control-oriented learning provides an accurate and practical framework for soft actuator control.

</details>


### [326] [CRESSim-Neo: A Batched GPU Simulation Engine for Surgical Robotics and Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25192)
*Yafei Ou,Ahnaf Naheen,Tleukhan Mussin,Hans Jarales,Melwin Moncy,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CRESSim-Neo，一个用于手术机器人和机器人学习的批量GPU仿真引擎，结合了刚体、可变形组织、流体和绳线的基于位置的仿真，以及批量渲染、手术特定传感和GPU驻留数据管线，在RTX 4090上实现了高达203万环境步/秒的性能，并扩展到涉及组织变形、流体交互和超声传感的批量手术场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人和机器人学习需要高速、可扩展且逼真的仿真环境，以支持大规模并行训练和合成数据生成。现有仿真器通常在CPU上运行或缺乏手术特定的传感与物理建模能力，无法满足GPU驻留的机器人学习和批量并行仿真的需求。

Method: CRESSim-Neo将刚体、可变形组织、流体和绳线的基于位置动力学(PBD)仿真与批量渲染、手术特定传感（如超声图像合成）以及GPU驻留数据管线相结合。通过允许直接访问物理和渲染缓冲区，实现零拷贝PyTorch集成（基于DLPack），从而支持GPU驻留的机器人学习。引擎支持组织操纵、流体抽吸、缝合、缆索驱动机器人和超声图像合成等手术应用。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 4090上，该引擎对8192个并行CartPole环境实现了高达203万环境步/秒的性能，并成功扩展到包含组织变形、流体交互和超声传感的批量手术场景。在刚体、变形体和流体仿真任务中均有验证，包括基于视觉和手术机器人学习场景。

Conclusion: CRESSim-Neo为手术仿真、合成数据生成和手术机器人学习提供了一个统一且可扩展的平台，填补了现有仿真器在GPU驻留数据管线和大规模批量并行仿真方面的空白。

Abstract: We introduce CRESSim-Neo, a batched GPU simulation engine for surgical robotics and robot learning. CRESSim-Neo combines position-based simulation of rigid bodies, deformable tissues, fluids, and strands with batched rendering, surgery-specific sensing, and a GPU-resident data pipeline. The engine supports applications including tissue manipulation, fluid suction, suturing, cable-driven robots, and ultrasound image synthesis. Direct access to physics and rendering buffers enables GPU-resident robot learning and zero-copy PyTorch integration using DLPack. We demonstrate CRESSim-Neo across rigid-body, deformable-body, and fluid simulation tasks, including vision-based and surgical robot-learning scenarios. On an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the engine achieves up to 2.03 million environment steps per second for 8192 parallel CartPole environments, and scales to batched surgical scenarios involving tissue deformation, fluid interaction, and ultrasound sensing. Overall, CRESSim-Neo provides a unified and scalable platform for surgical simulation, synthetic data generation, and surgical robot learning.

</details>


### [327] [Longitudinal Robot Learning from Demonstration with Care Providers in a Home Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25196)
*Nina Moorman,Julianna Schalkwyk,Vriksha Srihari,Qingyu Xiao,Kamel Alrashedy,Hongseok Jeong,Kiersten Lange,Matthew B. Luebbers,Matthew Gombolay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了非专家终端用户在没有实时机器人专家反馈的家庭环境中通过示范教学机器人的主要障碍，通过在护理人员群体中进行多次访问的人体实验来评估生态效度，并计划开源所收集的LfD数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LfD（从示范中学习）方法使非专家用户无需编程即可教机器人新技能，但对非专家用户可用性的评估大多在受控实验室环境中进行且有机器人专家在场。然而实际应用中，非专家用户需要在家庭环境中独立使用LfD，因此需要识别他们在没有实时专家反馈情况下教学机器人时面临的关键障碍。

Method: 通过人体受试者实验，在家庭环境中让护理人员群体作为非专家终端用户教机器人辅助任务，使用先前开发的两种示范者引导形式（预训练和自适应反馈）来支持用户，实验在多次访问中展开以增强生态效度。

Result: 识别了非专家终端用户在无实时机器人专家反馈的家庭环境中通过示范教学机器人的关键障碍，并生成了护理人员在多次家庭访问中教机器人辅助任务的LfD数据集，该数据集计划开源。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将LfD评估从实验室迁移到真实家庭环境并针对护理人员群体，提高了LfD可用性研究的生态效度，同时开源数据集可促进该领域的进一步研究与复现。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) methods enable non-expert end users to teach robots novel skills without explicit programming. However most evaluations of the usability of LfD with non-experts has been conducted in controlled laboratory environments with a robotics experimenter present. In this work we identify non-expert end users' key barriers when teaching robots via demonstration without live robotics expert feedback in a home environment. In our human subjects experiment we support the non-expert end users through two forms of demonstrator guidance developed in prior work: pre-training and adaptive feedback. Towards the ecological validity of the evaluation, we conduct this experimentation over multiple visits, with a population of care providers. Finally, we propose to open source the resulting LfD dataset of care providers teaching a robot assistive tasks over multiple visits to a home environment.

</details>


### [328] [Development of a Voice-Controlled Tendon-Driven Bionic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25222)
*Urja Kohli,Shagata Chanda,Kritika Gandhi,Charu Nigam,Aditi Surya Kamal,Pooja Bhati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一种基于肌腱驱动和Arduino微控制器的自动化仿生手，通过伺服电机实现手指协调运动，支持语音命令控制多种手势（张开、握拳、捏合、半屈曲），实验验证了其稳定的抓握能力和可重复的控制行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部损伤会严重影响个人日常生活能力，因此设计稳定且可控的辅助支撑设备是重要的研究领域。现有仿生手装置在结构复杂性和控制稳定性方面存在不足，需要更简化高效的设计方案。

Method: 采用肌腱驱动方法，由伺服电机配合校准的角控制来驱动手指运动；使用基于Arduino的微控制器进行编程控制，实现预设手势（张开手、握拳、捏合、半屈曲）；系统还集成了基于蓝牙收发器架构的语音命令接口，可即时将训练命令转换为手指动作。

Result: 实验测试表明：手指完成全序列屈曲动作约需7-8秒；系统在多个致动周期中表现出可重复且稳定的运动，无明显张力损失或控制精度下降；对不同形状和大小的物体均能稳定抓握，表明手指间协调一致。

Conclusion: 该仿生手系统提供了可预测且稳定的控制行为，同时具备简单高效的机械和控制架构，验证了所提设计方案的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: The impairment of the hands can seriously affect the abilities of every individual to perform the every-day activity, so the design of stable and controllable support devices is a significant field of study. This paper is about the design and implementation of an automated bionic hand which is dedicated to the coordinated finger movement through the simplified and efficient actuation mechanism. The method that the proposed system was designed on is the tendon-based method whereby the servo motors generate the movement of the fingers, with assistance of the angular control which is calibrated. An actuation is controlled by a microcontroller that will be programmed by use of an Arduino-based microcontroller to carry out programmed gestures that include open hand, fist, pinch and half flexion. It has an interface that is voice command enabled to make it easy to interact with a Bluetooth based sender receiver architecture which offers an option of executing trained commands which are immediately converted to finger actions. To explore the motions behavior, finger coordination and control response to the input, the behavior of the experiment system is tested. The actuation of the fingers was found to take a total of about 7-8 seconds to achieve full flexion of all fingers in a sequence. The system showed repetitive and constant motion throughout several actuation cycles without loss of any apparent tension or precision of control. There was a stable grasp of objects of different shapes and sizes, which implied consistent coordination between the fingers. These findings indicate that the proposed system offers predictable and steady control behavior and has a simple and efficient mechanical and control architecture.

</details>


### [329] [Generative Action-Chunk Sampling for Adaptive Stiffness Control in Physical Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25284)
*Aoi Otake,Ferdinand Hartmann,Ko Igari,Shingo Murata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式动作块采样的自适应刚度框架，用于物理人机协作。该策略以RGB图像和外部关节扭矩为条件，从观测先验中采样多个未来动作块，利用采样动作块间的变异度在线调节关节刚度和阻尼：变异大时更顺从以利于人类引导，变异小时提供更坚定的协助。在四种方向的协作搬运任务中，该方法平均成功率达0.95，优于固定刚度消融（0.83）和确定性基线（0.69）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机协作需要机器人在人类意图明确时提供主动协助，但在多种未来动作都可能发生时保持顺从。现有方法难以在协助与顺从之间动态平衡，需要一种能根据人类意图不确定性自适应调节机器人刚度的方法。

Method: 提出自适应刚度框架：(1) 以RGB图像和外部关节扭矩估计为条件，通过生成策略从观测条件先验中采样多个未来动作块；(2) 计算采样动作块之间的变异度；(3) 利用变异度连续调节关节刚度和阻尼——变异度高时增加顺从性以促进人类引导，变异度低时提供更坚定的协助。

Result: 在四种可能方向的真实协作搬运任务中，所提方法平均成功率为0.95，固定刚度消融为0.83，确定性基线为0.69。在接近方向决策点时，采样动作块间的变异度增大，控制器相应降低刚度，验证了方法的自适应性。

Conclusion: 生成式策略采样动作的变异度可作为在线控制信号，用于在物理人机交互中平衡协助与顺从。该方法为利用生成模型的固有不确定性来指导机器人阻抗调节提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Physical human-robot collaboration requires a robot to provide assistance when human intention is clear while remaining compliant when several future motions are plausible. We present an adaptive stiffness framework based on generative action-chunk sampling. Conditioned on an RGB image and external joint-torque estimates, the policy samples multiple future action chunks from an observation-conditioned prior. Variation among the sampled action chunks is used to continuously adapt joint stiffness and damping. Greater variation makes the robot more compliant to facilitate human guidance, whereas lower variation provides firmer assistance. In a real-world collaborative transport task with four possible directions, the proposed method achieved an average success rate of 0.95, compared with 0.83 for a fixed-stiffness ablation and 0.69 for a deterministic baseline. Near direction determination, variation among the sampled action chunks increased and the controller accordingly reduced stiffness. These results suggest that variation among actions sampled by a generative policy can serve as an online control signal for balancing assistance and compliance in physical human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [330] [RAEM: Robust Autonomous Exploration for Multi-Floor Environments with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25366)
*Zikang Yuan,Yuan Ren,Yian Wang,Yixue Wang,Enze Fang,Xuewei Zhang,Junda Cheng,Chi Chen,Chin-Pang Ho,Lijun Zhu,Shaohang Xu,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEM，一种面向多楼层环境的四足机器人鲁棒自主探索框架。它采用局部-全局混合可遍历性表示（局部层析图+局部3D网格图+全局拓扑图），解决现有平面化表示无法表达跨楼层连通性的问题，并通过楼梯中心对齐策略和双路径搜索机制保障探索的鲁棒性和计算稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面机器人探索方法依赖平面可遍历性表示，无法充分刻画多楼层建筑的重叠结构与跨楼层连通性；基于层析图的全局表示虽能有效建模多楼层导航，但在线探索中频繁重规划时维护全局层析图的计算开销过大；此外，楼梯井中稀疏且碎片化的LiDAR观测会降低局部可遍历性估计质量，导致视点放置不规则和临时拓扑断连。

Method: RAEM采用混合局部-全局可遍历性表示：局部层析图和显式分类的局部3D网格图用于在线地形分析与连通性评估，高程感知的全局拓扑图由局部空间表示增量构建，用于高效的跨楼层探索规划。同时引入楼梯中心对齐策略减少攀爬时的突发偏航变化，以及双路径搜索机制在全局拓扑局部断连时恢复引导路径。

Result: 大量仿真与实物实验表明，RAEM在多楼层结构中实现了鲁棒且计算稳定的自主探索，包括连续探索五层楼梯井场景。

Conclusion: RAEM通过混合表示与针对性策略，有效解决了多楼层环境中平面表示不足、全局层析图计算开销大以及楼梯场景局部观测退化等问题，验证了其在多楼层自主探索任务中的实用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RAEM, a robust autonomous exploration framework for quadruped robots operating in multi-floor environments. Most existing ground-robot exploration approaches rely on planar traversability representations, which cannot adequately represent the overlapping structures and cross-floor connectivity of multi-floor buildings. Although tomography-based representations provide effective traversability modeling for multi-floor navigation, maintaining a global tomography map incurs substantial computational overhead for online exploration with frequent replanning. Moreover, sparse and fragmented LiDAR observations in stairwells can degrade local traversability estimation, leading to irregular viewpoint placement and temporary topological disconnections. To address these challenges, RAEM adopts a hybrid local-global traversability representation, in which a local tomography map and an explicitly categorized local 3D grid map are used for online terrain analysis and connectivity evaluation, while an elevation-aware global topological graph is incrementally constructed from these local spatial representations for efficient cross-floor exploration planning. We further introduce a staircase center alignment strategy to reduce abrupt yaw variations during climbing and a dual path searching mechanism to recover guidance paths when the global topology is locally disconnected. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate robust and computationally stable autonomous exploration across multi-floor structures, including continuous exploration of a five-floor stairwell.

</details>


### [331] [A Taxonomy of Construction Task Activities for Robot Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25395)
*Sadman Sakib,Zhangyi None Peng,Yujie Pang,Yu Otsuki,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TARCAT是一个基于职业（occupation-grounded）的施工机器人动作分类体系，从91个O*NET任务和30个教学视频中提炼出41个动作原语（分为12组和3类），支持将参数化原语序列组合为可复用技能，并在DOBOT CR3机械臂上演示了部分原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型虽能扩展到更广泛的任务，但施工部署需要精确梳理工人活动及其所需能力，缺乏一个统一的、可解释的动作词汇表来组织示范数据、明确机器人需求并支持技能库的检索与扩展。

Method: 从7个高就业施工职业的91个O*NET任务以及30个物理工作教学视频中提取动作，构建TARCAT分类体系，定义41个动作原语（12组、3类），并提供将参数化原语序列组合成可复用技能的机制；在DOBOT CR3臂+CRAFT手上验证了部分原语。

Result: 提出了一套包含41个动作原语、12组和3类的可解释分类体系；展示了参数化原语组合为技能库的机制；在DOBOT CR3机械臂上成功演示了选定原语；发布了公开标注数据与代码。

Conclusion: TARCAT为分析人类施工工作和开发通用施工机器人提供了共同词汇，有助于衔接人类示范与机器人执行，推动通用施工机器人发展。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action models offer a path toward robots with broader repertoires than conventional task-specific systems. Construction deployment, however, requires a precise inventory of worker activities and the capabilities needed to execute them. We present TARCAT, an occupation-grounded taxonomy derived from 91 O*NET tasks across seven high-employment construction occupations and 30 instructional videos of physical work. TARCAT defines 41 action primitives in 12 groups and three classes and provides a mechanism for composing parameterized primitive sequences into reusable skills. This human-interpretable structure can organize demonstrations, specify robot requirements, and support coding agents that retrieve and extend skill libraries.
